reset<br />
load<br />
break main

</div></div><div class="thread-post"><h3 class="thread-post-title">FreeRTOS causes SIGINT on STM32</h3><div class="thread-post-author">Posted by <a href="http://sourceforge.net/users/kenan3">kenan3</a> on June 8, 2010</div><div class="thread-post-content">Let me try to explain to you what I&#8217;m trying to do.  
There was a simple led blink example for the stm32. The original example which is working without freeRTOS is working fine.&nbsp; So I tooked that code and put it into a task. In my main routine I just call the taskCreate and after that the startscheduler funktions.   
After that I tried to run the code, but the instead the the blinking LED I received a SIGINT. So I decided to debug that to figure out why I received a SIGINT. On this way I found out that the reason for the SIGINT was a the call of memset&#40;&#41; in task.c of freeRTOS. I know now that is it not a FreeRTOS problem, but at the first time I thought so.   
I think it has something to do with the toolchain. So I will try to find out what it might be.  
But anyway thx for your help..
